# AST-842 — Database updates are not running on production deployments

<!-- linear-archive: AST-842 archived 2026-07-29 -->

## Linear archive (AST-842)

**Archived:** 2026-07-29  
**Linear URL:** https://linear.app/astralcareermatch/issue/AST-842/database-updates-are-not-running-on-production-deployments  
**Status at archive:** Archive  
**Project:** Astral Foundation  
**Assignee:** chuckles  
**Priority / estimate:** Urgent / —  
**Parent:** —  
**Blocked by / blocks / related:** related: AST-883

### Description

## Purpose

Production Railway deployments can run scheduled work against a SQLite `company` table that lags the canonical schema the running code expects. Schema changes today apply only when lazy ensure runs on first access to a table in a given process—not at deploy or bootstrap—so a table can stay stale until a code path that triggers ensure runs successfully. Susan observed `inflow_discovery` succeeding locally but failing in production; production `PRAGMA table_info(company)` omits `batch_created_at`, which localhost has. Company batch claim and dispatch paths use that column; without it, production behavior diverges from local. This epic closes the production schema-drift gap so every deploy brings persistent tables to the current canonical layout before scheduled dispatch runs.

## Functional scope

* **Confirm production vs canonical drift:** Verify production `company` is missing `batch_created_at` (and any other columns the current codebase expects beyond Susan's diff); document observed delta and tie it to the inflow failure symptoms.
* **Bootstrap-time schema ensure:** On every server process start, before the scheduler runs dispatch, run idempotent schema ensure for the table set Susan confirms—so production databases upgrade automatically on deploy/restart, not only when upsert or a rare code path touches a table first.
* **Complete company lazy migrations:** Ensure the company-table ensure path adds every canonical column missing on legacy databases (including `batch_created_at`), matching localhost layout after bootstrap.
* **Legacy column retirement:** Remove `agent_responses_legacy` from the canonical company schema once safe—migrate or discard any remaining values per Susan's direction, and stop creating or requiring the column on fresh databases.
* **Deploy verification:** After a production deploy/restart, Susan can confirm `company` matches canonical columns and `inflow_discovery` runs without schema-related errors.

## Boundaries

* Does **not** refactor the data-layer module structure or relocate migrations—that is [AST-777](https://linear.app/astralcareermatch/issue/AST-777/refactor-databasepy).
* Does **not** change upsert merge rules, Data Management UI, or table upsert validation semantics from AST-373/626/637.
* Does **not** add new tables, change SQLite engine, or alter inflow_discovery business logic beyond what schema repair unblocks.
* Does **not** drop other legacy columns unless Susan explicitly adds them to scope.
* Must **not** break existing local dev databases, Railway staging, or production DBs during additive migrations; destructive drops require explicit Susan approval and a data-safe path.
* Per Code Rules, the data-layer table inventory header remains authoritative; any canonical column change updates that inventory.

## Acceptance criteria

1. After a fresh production deploy/restart, `PRAGMA table_info(company)` on Railway production includes `batch_created_at` and all other canonical columns from Susan's localhost reference (minus any legacy columns explicitly retired in scope).
2. `agent_responses_legacy` is absent from fresh databases and removed or empty on upgraded production without data loss Susan has not approved.
3. `inflow_discovery` dispatch completes on production for a candidate that succeeds on localhost with the same codebase version (no schema-column errors in logs or dispatch ledger).
4. Bootstrap schema ensure runs once per process before the scheduler starts dispatch work—not only on upsert or first ad-hoc query.
5. Bootstrap ensure is idempotent on databases already at canonical schema; local and staging behave the same after restart.
6. Susan can compare production and localhost column lists post-deploy and see them match (except retired legacy columns).

## Dependencies and blockers

* [AST-841](https://linear.app/astralcareermatch/issue/AST-841/inflow-discovery-failed-status-vs-batch-log-alignment-filter-execution) (inflow_discovery FAILED status vs batch log alignment) — User Testing; orthogonal logging alignment, but production schema must be sound to validate end-to-end.
* [AST-777](https://linear.app/astralcareermatch/issue/AST-777/refactor-databasepy) (Refactor [database.py](<http://database.py>)) — Discussion; child 2 targets explicit bootstrap long-term; overlaps this epic—Susan decides tactical ship vs wait.

## Open questions

1. Bootstrap scope: ensure **company** only (minimum to unblock inflow), or **all** tables in the upsert schema registry on every startup?
   1. all tables on every startup.  (DO NOT REPLACE DATA, JUST ENSURE SCHEMA for all tables)
2. [AST-777](https://linear.app/astralcareermatch/issue/AST-777/refactor-databasepy) **overlap:** Ship bootstrap ensure as a tactical fix here, or block [AST-842](https://linear.app/astralcareermatch/issue/AST-842/database-updates-are-not-running-on-production-deployments) until [AST-777](https://linear.app/astralcareermatch/issue/AST-777/refactor-databasepy) child 2 lands?
   1. [AST-777](https://linear.app/astralcareermatch/issue/AST-777/refactor-databasepy) will supersede this change, but it may be deferred and we need this fix now.
3. **Legacy drop:** Confirm `agent_responses_legacy` is the only column to drop; should any non-empty values be migrated to `agent_responses` before drop?
   1. Ignore the legacy column for now, we'll clean up with 777.
4. **Production recovery:** Does Susan want a documented one-time repair on the current Railway DB before deploy, or rely solely on the next restart after code ships?
   1. No, just with every server restart.

## Original brief

Since the inflow_discovery task runs fine on localhost but fails on production, I suspect there is a missing update to the company table definition that did not get asserted on boot.

This is the table definition for company on localhost. Please confirm this is the case, and see if we can't drop legacy columns as named here:

```
🔑 short_name TEXT
state TEXT
company_name TEXT
company_website TEXT
job_site TEXT
batch_id TEXT
batch_created_at TIMESTAMP
company_data TEXT
agent_responses TEXT
created_at TIMESTAMP
updated_at TIMESTAMP
state_updated_at TIMESTAMP
candidate_id TEXT
last_scan_at TIMESTAMP
state_history TEXT
agent_responses_legacy TEXT
```

Here is the table definition in production:

```
🔑 short_name TEXT
state TEXT
company_name TEXT
company_website TEXT
job_site TEXT
batch_id TEXT
company_data TEXT
agent_responses TEXT
created_at TIMESTAMP
updated_at TIMESTAMP
state_updated_at TIMESTAMP
candidate_id TEXT
last_scan_at TIMESTAMP
state_history TEXT
agent_responses_legacy TEXT
```

| **AST-846** | bootstrap crashes creating job unique index on production duplicates |

### Quick re-test (this fix wave only)

Confirm we understood each issue — **not** a full epic regression pass.

**AST-846** — _bootstrap crashes creating job unique index on production duplicates_
- **Issue reported:** After AST-843 landed on Railway production, the gunicorn worker fails to boot on every restart. Bootstrap calls `ensure_all_upsert_registry_schemas_at_startup()` → `_ensure_job_schema`, which raises `sqlite3.IntegrityError: UNIQUE constraint failed: job.company, job.job_title, jo
- **Should now:** Server process starts successfully on production after deploy/restart. Bootstrap schema ensure completes idempotently even when the production SQLite file contains legacy duplicate job rows. Schema migration must not crash the worker.
- **Quick check (this fix only):**
  1. Deploy AST-842/843 to Railway production (commit on origin/dev with bootstrap schema ensure).
  2. Observe Railway deploy logs after container start.
  3. Worker crashes during `bootstrap_runtime()` with `IntegrityError` on job unique index creation in `_ensure_job_schema`.

Local `dev` merged via prep-uat. Staging should reflect the fixes above.

— Chuckles
